Let's aim for an optimizer that uses both direct a (simplex) and LSE with adaptive beta.

Detailed optimizer idea:
- a length n, sum=1.
- At each iteration:
  - c = conv(a)
  - cur = c.max()
  - Update best if cur<best.
  - Choose weights w: if phase=smooth, softmax with beta (logsumexp weights); if phase=peak, top-k weights proportional to c sorted.

Backtracking line search details:
```
def try_step(a, v, cur, best_score, rng):
    amax = min(a_i / max(-v_i,0)) for v_i<0 else inf
    alpha = min(0.5*amax, alpha_base)
    for _ in range(tries):
        an = project_simplex(a + alpha*v, 1)
        cn = conv(an); val=cn.max()
        if val < cur - 1e-12:
            return an,val,alpha,improved
        alpha *= 0.5
    return None
```
If no improvement at very small alpha, direction may be zero; skip.

```
  iteration++
  c=conv(a); val=c.max()
  if val<best_val: best_val=val; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0; maybe update base_alpha*1.1
  else: no_improve++

  # If no improvement for long, use best as start with noise
  if no_improve > 200:
      a = best_a.copy()
      # add smooth noise: low frequencies or random
      noise = rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n)
      noise = lowpass? Could use cumulative? Use random Fourier: noise_fft? Simpler: random walk? Smooth noise: generate freq coefficients [0, random low freq]. We can do `noise = rng.normal(0,1,n)` then cumulative twice? To keep smooth: invfft of random low-frequency? Maybe overkill. Use small random uniform on subset.
      a = project_simplex(a + noise,1)
      base_alpha=0.2
      c=conv(a); val=c.max()
  # select peaks
  K adaptive: starts 1, increases with no_improve? More peaks if stuck. K = min(256, 4 + no_improve//10?)
  if K small: top indices via argpartition
  p = top values / top sum or random dirichlet
  # choose weights scheme randomly:
  r=rng.random()
  if r<0.5: weights = c[idx]/sum
  elif r<0.7: weights = ones/K
  else: weights = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))
  g=zeros(n)
  for each k,w: add shifted a contribution
  # project tangent: mean g maybe 2*sum a? For constraint sum fixed, g_t=g - mean(g)
  gt = g - np.mean(g)
  v = -gt
  # If v norm zero, noise.
  # alpha_max:
  neg=v<0; if any: amax=min(a[neg]/(-v[neg])); else amax=1.0
  alpha=min(base_alpha, 0.5*amax)
  # if alpha too small <1e-10, use projection and restart
  for tries in range(10):
      cand = a + alpha*v
      # if min <0 due to alpha maybe not; but clip and renormalize (sum same)
      if np.any(cand <0): cand = np.clip(cand,0,None); cand/=cand.sum()
      cn=conv(cand); valn=cn.max()
      if valn < val - 1e-12:
          a=cand; base_alpha=min(1.0, base_alpha*1.05)
          no_improve=0
          break
      alpha *=0.5
  else:
      # no descent; try different weights/noise
      base_alpha*=0.7
      no_improve++
      if no_improve > 500:
         # random smooth perturb and set a; maybe accept worse up to temp
```

```
    n=len(a)
```

```
def top_indices(c, K):
   m=len(c)
   if K>=m: return np.arange(m)
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   order=np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
   return idx[order]
```
- `project_simplex(v,S)`: standard, return S v/sum? Actually projects onto sum=S nonnegative. S=1. If S is scalar. Use sort.
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v,float)
    if n==0: return v
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum=np.cumsum(w)
    rho=np.where(w > (cum-S)/np.arange(1,n+1))[0]
    if len(rho)==0:
       theta=S/n
    else:
       rho=rho[-1]? Need max valid? Standard: find largest i such that w_i > (cumsum_i-S)/i.
```
Let's implement robust:
```
rho = np.arange(1,n+1)
support = w > (cum - S)/rho
if not support.any():
   theta=S/n
else:
   rho_idx = np.max(np.where(support)[0])
   theta = (cum[rho_idx]-S)/(rho_idx+1)
u=v-theta
u[u<0]=0
# adjust numerical drift
return u*(S/ u.sum()) if u.sum()>0 else np.full(n,S/n)
```
Note if all v zero and S=1, u sum after proj maybe 1? We'll renormalize.

- `low_freq_noise(rng, n, scale)`: generate smooth noise. We can use random Fourier: create `f = np.fft.rfft(rng.normal(0,1,n))`, zero high freq, inverse. Simpler: `noise = rng.normal(0,1,n); noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(min(n,128))/128, 'same')` but convolution O(n). Use cumulative sum twice? Could just use small uniform on random contiguous blocks. For escape, smooth noise helpful. Implement:
```
def smooth_noise(rng, n, scale):
    m = max(4, min(n, 512))
    # random coefficients for first m frequencies
    r = rng.normal(0,1,m)
    # r ifft maybe complex; use real
    f = np.zeros(n//2+1)
    f[:m] = r
    noise = np.fft.irfft(f, n)
    return noise * (scale / (max(1e-12, np.linalg.norm(noise)) * np.sqrt(n)))? maybe to relative std.
```
Since r normalization, std ~√(m/n), scale directly maybe. Use `scale=0.02*S/√n`? In previous code noise scale* S /√n. For sum1, scale relative maybe 0.05/√n. We can set noise std = 0.05/√(2n)? Actually adding std σ to each a (sum1) changes relative O(σ√n). Choose σ = 0.01/√n gives variance total 0.0001, small. For bigger escape, 0.05/√n. Implement smoothing `noise = rng.normal(0, 0.02/np.sqrt(n), n)` (white) maybe enough. Smooth white not necessary.

But in local search, random perturb should be accepted if within tolerance to escape; might degrade best. Use: `pert = best_a.copy(); pert += noise; pert=project; val=conv(pert); if val < best_val*1.01/1.001: a=pert else a=best_a`. Temperature anneal.

Let's flesh core:
```
def search_kernel(a_init, tag, deadline, rng, start_beta? maybe):
    a = normalize_one(a_init.copy())
    c = conv_fft(a); val = float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy(); best_val=val
    base_alpha=0.4
    no_improve=0; total_iter=0; stuck_noise=0
    Kmin=2; Kmax=128
    c = conv_fft(a) # need for peak
    while time < deadline-1:
       total_iter+=1
       c = conv_fft(a); val = float(c.max())
       if val < best_val - 1e-12:
          best_val=val; best_a=a.copy(); base_alpha=min(1.5, base_alpha*1.05); no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 800 and (slow? new pert):
          # small jump from best
          noise_scale = 0.02 / np.sqrt(n) * (1+min(5,no_improve/2000))
          jump = best_a.copy()
          noise = rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n)
          # smoothing? maybe np.convolve with small kernel
          jump = project_simplex(jump + noise, 1.0)
          c=conv(jump); valj=...
          if valj < best_val * 1.05:
              a = jump; val=valj
          base_alpha=0.2
          no_improve=0
       # Determine K: if val close to best and no_improve high, use larger K
       K = int(np.min(128, np.max(2, 4 + no_improve//50)))
       K = min(K, max(1,n))
       idx = top_indices(c, K)
       # choose weights
       r = rng.random()
       cp = c[idx]
       if r < 0.25:
          # heaviest single top
          w = np.zeros(K); w[0]=1.0
       elif r < 0.5:
          s=cp.sum(); w=cp/s if s>0 else np.ones(K)/K
       elif r < 0.7:
          w=np.ones(K)/K
       else:
          w=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))
       g=grad_top(a, idx, w)
       # subtract mean
       gt = g - g.mean()
       v = -gt
       vn = np.linalg.norm(v)
       if vn < 1e-14:
           # perturb
           continue
       # alpha max
       neg = v < 0
       if np.any(neg):
          amax = np.min(a[neg] / (-v[neg]))
       else:
          amax = 1.0
       alpha = min(base_alpha, 0.5*amax)
       if alpha < 1e-12:
          # projection step? maybe add negative? set a=best
          alpha = amax*0.9? if amax>0 else 1e-6
       # Line search
       improved=False
       for tries in range(12):
           cand = a + alpha*v
           # ensure nonnegative and sum1
           minc = cand.min()
           if minc < 0 or abs(cand.sum()-1)>1e-9:
               cand = project_simplex(cand, 1.0)
           cn=conv_fft(cand); valn=float(cn.max())
           if valn < val - 1e-12:
              a=cand; val=valn; improved=True
              # Increase alpha if accepted after few tries? base_alpha adjusted externally via no_improve
              break
           alpha *= 0.5
       if not improved:
          base_alpha *= 0.8
          # Occasionally try projected random direction?
       # Periodically verify best internal? not needed
       # Safety: if global objective from evaluate_sequence finite? not inside.
    return best_a, best_val
```

Will this make fast? Each iter: conv (2 rfft size 2n), top argpartition O(n), grad_top loops K (K up to 128). If K grows 128, grad 128 slice additions; maybe heavy. But K only grows when stuck; can cap 32. Use Kmax=32.

Top argpartition each iter over 2n=16k relatively cheap. `np.argpartition` plus sorting K. Good.

```

def run_hill(a_init, rng, start_time, limit_time, Kmax=32, explore=0.2, tag='hill'):
    n = len(a_init)
    a = normalize_one(np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64))
    c = conv_self(a)
    val = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy(); best_val = val
    base_alpha = 0.25
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    print(f"[{tag}] start n={n} score={2*n*val:.6f}")
    obj_initial = 2*n*val
    while time.monotonic() < limit_time - 0.1:
       it += 1
       c = conv_self(a); val = float(c.max())
       if val < best_val - 1e-13:
           best_val = val; best_a = a.copy(); no_improve=0
           # if big improvement, maybe increase alpha
           base_alpha = min(1.0, base_alpha*1.05 + 0.01)
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       if it == 1000 or (time.monotonic()-last_print)>30:
           print(f"[{tag}] it={it} obj={2*n*val:.6f} best={2*n*best_val:.6f} alpha={base_alpha:.4f}")
           last_print=time.monotonic()
       # jump if stuck
       if no_improve > 1200 or (no_improve > 300 and (it % 500 == 0 and rng.random()<0.3)):
           jump_score = best_val
           for _ in range(3):
               scale = (0.01 + 0.05*(no_improve/2000.0)) / np.sqrt(n)
               jump = best_a + rng.normal(0, scale, n)
               jump = project_simplex(jump, 1.0)
               vj = float(conv_self(jump).max())
               if vj < jump_score:
                   jump_score = vj
           # accept if improvement or with small temp
           temp = 1e-4 + 5e-4*(no_improve/2000.0)
           if jump_score < best_val * (1.0 + temp) or jump_score < best_val - 1e-13:
               # we need the actual jump that gave jump_score; recompute one or store
           ...
       # To accept jump, store best_jump array in loop.
```

Detailed helper implementation:

```
def normalize_one(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s
```
If input has large values up to1000, normalize to1. Some zero.

`conv_self`:
```
def conv_self(a):
    n = a.size
    size = 2*n - 1
    fsize = next_fast_len(size)
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(A*A, fsize)
    c = c[:size]
    # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    # renormalize due FFT error: sum(c) may be (sum a)^2 =1. If sum diff, scale c? To preserve exact sum maybe scaling reduces error.
    total = c.sum()
    if total > 0:
        c *= (1.0 / total)
    return c
```
Scaling c by 1/total may slightly alter max but improves consistency. Since sum a=1, sum c=1; FFT errors small. Do it. If total zero impossible.

`grad_from_peaks`:
```
def grad_from_peaks(a, idx, w):
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk, ww in zip(idx, w):
        k = int(kk)
        if ww == 0.0: continue
        if k < 0 or k >= 2*n-1: continue
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        js = k - lo
        je = k - hi
        seg = a[je:js+1][::-1]
        g[lo:hi+1] += (2.0*ww) * seg
    return g
```
`idx` may be np array int, w list. Good.

`top_indices`:
```
def top_indices(c, k):
    m = c.size
    k = int(min(max(1,k), m))
    if k == 1:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    order = np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
    return idx[order].astype(np.int64)
```
`np.argpartition(c,-k)` if k=m, works? It says kth must < size; if k=m, -m invalid? Actually `np.argpartition(c, -m)` works? kth index -m equals 0, okay. But to be safe if k>=m return np.arange.

`project_simplex`: Use standard. But with S=1. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0: return v
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = w > (cum - S) / idx
    if not np.any(rho):
        theta = S / n
    else:
        pos = int(np.where(rho)[0].max())
        theta = (cum[pos] - S) / (pos + 1)
    u = v - theta
    u = np.maximum(u, 0.0)
    su = u.sum()
    if su <= 0 or not np.isfinite(su):
        return np.full(n, S/n, dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(su - S) > 1e-12:
        u *= S / su
    return u
```
Check support condition: We need largest i where w_i > (cum_i - S)/i. Yes.

Generating initializations:
```
def make_power(n, alpha=1.0, p=0.5):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = (t + max(1e-3, alpha)) ** (-p)
    return normalize_one(a)

def make_exponential(n, alpha=1.0, fwd=True):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    if fwd: a = np.exp(alpha * t)
    else: a = np.exp(alpha * (1.0 - t))
    return normalize_one(a)

def make_smooth_random(rng, n, nfreq=8, amp=0.5):
    m = max(2, min(n//2+1, nfreq+1))
    f = np.zeros(n//2+1, dtype=np.float64)
    f[1:m] = rng.normal(0, 1.0, m-1)
    noise = irfft(f, n)
    mean = noise.mean(); noise -= mean
    s = noise.std()
    if s > 1e-15: noise = amp * noise / s
    else: noise = 0
    a = 1.0 + noise
    return normalize_one(a)
```
If amp small, positive. Good.

`make_sin`:
```
def make_sin(n, m=1, amp=0.3, phase=0.0):
    t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
    a=1.0+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t+phase)
    return normalize_one(np.maximum(a,0))
```

`downsample`:
```
def downsample(a, factor):
    a = np.asarray(a)
    if factor <=1: return normalize_one(a)
    out = a[0::factor]
    return normalize_one(out)
```

`interp_seq`:
```
def interp_seq(a, new_n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    old_n=a.size
    if old_n == new_n: return normalize_one(a)
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_one(out)
```

```
       # if n_base > 16384: base = interp_seq(parent, 16384) else parent
```

`smooth_noise` implementation:
```
def smooth_noise(rng, n, scale):
    m = max(2, min(n//2+1, 16))
    f = np.zeros(n//2+1, dtype=np.float64)
    f[1:m] = rng.normal(0, 1.0, m-1)
    z = irfft(f, n)
    z -= z.mean()
    sd = z.std()
    if sd > 0:
        z = z * (scale / (sd * np.sqrt(n)))? Wait want total L2 norm? If adding to a sum1, we want typical value change scale maybe 0.01/√n. If z normalized std1, set std = 0.01/√n? Then total relative std ~0.01.
```
Simpler in jump: `noise = rng.normal(0, noise_std, n)` with noise_std = 0.01/√n. White. For smooth, use `noise = smooth_noise(rng,n,0.01)` where smooth_noise returns array with std = scale/√n? Let's implement:
```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,relative=0.01):
    # returns zero-mean noise with per-element std approx relative/sqrt(n)
    m=...
    z=irfft(...)
    z-=z.mean()
    sd=z.std()
    if sd>0: z*= (relative/np.sqrt(n))/sd
    return z
```

```
Need `softmax_weights` top-level:
```
def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    cm = c.max()
    z = beta*(c-cm)
    # z <=0. If cm==0 return None
    ew = np.exp(z)
    s = ew.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return None
    return ew/s
```
If beta large and c-cm negative large, ew underflows all zeros. Then fallback.

`grad_weights_fft`:
```
def grad_weights_fft(a, w):
    n = a.size
    if w.size != 2*n-1: ...
    fsize = next_fast_len(w.size + n - 1)
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return g
```
This uses complex FFT, maybe slower but fine. Import `fft, ifft`.
```

```
Pseudo-code:
```
def run_hill(a_init, rng, start_time, limit_time, Kmax=48, explore=0.1, tag=''):
    n = int(a_init.size)
    a = normalize_one(a_init.copy())
    c = conv_self(a); val=float(c.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_val=val
    base_alpha=0.2
    no_improve=0; it=0; last_print=0
    tag_obj=2*n*val
    print(...)
    while time.monotonic() < limit_time - 0.1:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= limit_time: break
       # periodically print
       c=conv_self(a); val=float(c.max()); it+=1
       if val < best_val - 1e-13:
           best_val=val; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0; base_alpha=min(1.5, base_alpha*1.1+0.005)
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       if it%200==0: print...
       # Jump if stuck
       if no_improve > 1500:
           # 5 jumps
           best_jump=None; best_jump_val=best_val
           for _ in range(5):
              scale = min(0.08, 0.015 + 0.02*(no_improve/2000.0))
              jump = best_a + smooth_noise(rng,n,scale)
              jump = project_simplex(jump,1.0)
              vj=float(conv_self(jump).max())
              if vj < best_jump_val:
                 best_jump_val=vj; best_jump=jump.copy()
           temp=1e-3 + 5e-4*min(2.0,no_improve/2000.0)
           if best_jump is not None and best_jump_val < best_val*(1.0+temp):
              a=best_jump; val=best_jump_val; no_improve=0; base_alpha=0.1
           else:
              a=best_a.copy(); val=best_val; no_improve=0
       # Choose direction
       lse_prob = 0.03 + min(0.45, 0.01*no_improve/100.0) + 0.2*explore
       use_lse = (lse_prob > 1.0 ?) }
       if rng.random() < min(0.8,lse_prob):
           beta_factor = 20.0 + 10.0*min(20.0, no_improve/200.0)
           w = softmax_weights(c, beta_factor*n)
           if w is None: use_lse=False
       if use_lse:
           g = grad_weights_fft(a,w)
       else:
           K = int(min(Kmax, max(2, 2 + no_improve//200)))
           # ensure K at least count close if small
           maxv = c.max()
           # close count maybe expensive but okay if no_improve high
           if K < 16 and no_improve > 200:
              close = np.count_nonzero(c >= maxv - 1e-10)
              K = min(Kmax, max(K, int(close)))
           idx=top_indices(c,K)
           sp=c[idx]
           r=rng.random()
           if r<0.35:
              w=np.zeros(K); w[0]=1.0
           elif r<0.65:
              s=sp.sum(); w=sp/s if s>0 else np.ones(K)/K
           else:
              if rng.random()<0.5:
                 w=np.ones(K)/K
              else:
                 w=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))
           g=grad_from_peaks(a, idx, w)
       gt = g - g.mean()
       if np.linalg.norm(gt) < 1e-13:
           # random direction
           v = rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n); v -= v.mean()
       else:
           v = -gt
       # line search
       alpha=base_alpha
       improved=False; attempts=0
       for attempts in range(10):
           neg = v < 0
           if np.any(neg):
              # avoid huge by alpha max
              # compute amax each attempt? alpha changes, amax same independent of alpha (for linear in alpha): amax = min(a[neg]/(-v[neg]))
              # precompute outside
              ...

if np.any(neg): amm = np.min(a[neg]/(-v[neg])) else amm=1.0
use_proj = (amm < 1e-8) or alpha > amm
for attempts:
   if not use_proj:
      alpha = min(alpha, 0.95*amm)
      cand = a + alpha*v
      if cand.min() < 0: cand = project_simplex(cand,1.0); use_proj=True
      else: cand /= cand.sum()

```
Let's carefully write `run_hill` syntax.

```
def run_hill(a_init, rng, limit_time, Kmax=48, explore=0.1, tag='hill'):
    n = int(np.asarray(a_init).size)
    a = normalize_one(np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64))
    c = conv_self(a)
    val = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_val = val
    base_alpha = 0.2
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0.0
    print("[{}] n={} initial obj={:.8f}".format(tag,n,2*n*val), flush=True)
    while time.monotonic() < limit_time - 0.1:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= limit_time:
            break
        c = conv_self(a)
        val = float(c.max())
        it += 1
        if val < best_val - 1e-13:
            best_val = val
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            base_alpha = min(1.5, base_alpha * 1.05 + 0.005)
        if it % 200 == 0 or (now - last_print) > 30.0:
            print(...)
            last_print=now
        # Jump
        if stall > 1200:
            best_jump = None
            jump_val = best_val
            for jj in range(5):
                scale = min(0.08, 0.015 + 0.015 * min(3.0, float(stall)/1500.0))
                jump = best_a + smooth_noise(rng, n, scale)
                jump = project_simplex(jump, 1.0)
                vj = float(conv_self(jump).max())
                if vj < jump_val:
                    jump_val = vj
                    best_jump = jump.copy()
            temp = 1e-3 + 5e-4 * min(2.0, float(stall)/1500.0)
            if best_jump is not None and jump_val < best_val * (1.0 + temp):
                a = best_jump
                val = jump_val
                stall = 0
                base_alpha = 0.1
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()
                val = best_val
                stall = 0
        # choose direction
        lse_prob = 0.03 + min(0.45, 0.0005 * float(stall)) + 0.2 * explore
        # lse_prob cap 0.8
        use_lse = False
        if rng.random() < min(0.8, lse_prob):
            beta_factor = 25.0 + 10.0 * min(20.0, float(stall) / 250.0)
            w = softmax_weights(c, beta_factor * float(n))
            if w is not None:
                use_lse = True
        if use_lse:
            g = grad_weights_fft(a, w)
        else:
            K = int(min(Kmax, max(2, 2 + int(stall) // 200)))
            # close peaks if stall high
            if K < 16 and stall > 200:
                maxv = float(c.max())
                close_cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(c >= maxv - 1e-10))
                if close_cnt > K:
                    K = int(min(Kmax, close_cnt))
            idx = top_indices(c, K)
            sp = c[idx]
            r = rng.random()
            if r < 0.35:
                w = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
                w[0] = 1.0
            elif r < 0.65:
                s = float(sp.sum())
                if s > 0.0:
                    w = sp / s
                else:
                    w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
            else:
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
                else:
                    w = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64))
            g = grad_from_peaks(a, idx, w)
        gt = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gt))
        if gnorm < 1e-13:
            v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
        else:
            v = -gt
        neg = v < 0.0
        if np.any(neg):
            amax = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-v[neg])))
        else:
            amax = 1.0
        use_projection = (amax < 1e-8)
        alpha = base_alpha
        improved = False
        attempt = 0
        for attempt in range(10):
            if not use_projection:
                if alpha > 0.95 * amax:
                    alpha = 0.95 * amax
                if alpha <= 0.0:
                    alpha = 1e-8
                cand = a + alpha * v
                mc = float(cand.min())
                if mc < -1e-12:
                    cand = project_simplex(cand, 1.0)
                    use_projection = True
                else:
                    ss = float(cand.sum())
                    if ss > 0.0 and abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-10:
                        cand = cand / ss
                    elif mc < 0.0:
                        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                        cand = cand / cand.sum()
            else:
                cand = project_simplex(a + alpha * v, 1.0)
            cn = conv_self(cand)
            valn = float(cn.max())
            if valn < val - 1e-13:
                a = cand
                val = valn
                improved = True
                break
            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < 1e-10:
                break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if attempt == 0:
                base_alpha = min(2.0, base_alpha * 1.25 + 0.005)
            else:
                base_alpha = min(2.0, base_alpha * 1.05)
        else:
            stall += 1
            base_alpha *= 0.85
            if base_alpha < 1e-6:
                base_alpha = 1e-6
        # reset if badly lost
        if val > best_val * 1.02 and stall > 400:
            a = best_a.copy()
            val = best_val
            stall = 0
            base_alpha = 0.2
    obj = 2.0 * n * best_val
    print("[{}] final obj={:.8f}".format(tag, obj), flush=True)
    return best_a, obj
```
Potential bug: `attempt` variable if no attempt? initialized. Good.

`lse_prob`: `0.0005*stall`, if stall 1000 =>0.5. cap 0.8. Good.

The close peak count `np.count_nonzero(c >= maxv - 1e-10)`: If plateau many due uniform, K to Kmax (48). Fine.

`project_simplex` sorting in candidate if min slightly negative; may happen often if alpha not capped due use_projection false but after cancellation min -1e-12. It will project (sort) and set use_projection True for subsequent attempts same direction (though alpha halves next loop? use_projection remains true for rest line search). Good.

If use_projection true in first attempt, candidate = project; if no improve, alpha halves but still projection. Fine.

Line search failure due plateau may set base_alpha very small. Jumps eventually.
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(max(0.0, budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 0:
       # fallback quickly
    if budget_s < 120:
       parent = load_parent...
       if parent is not None: return ...
       return [1.0]*4096? normalized.
    deadline = start + budget_s - 30.0
    MAX_N=16384
    parent = load...
    if parent is not None and parent.size > MAX_N:
       parent = interp_seq(parent, MAX_N)
    if parent is None:
       parent = make_power(8192,1.0)
       parent_is_given=False
    else: parent_is_given=True
    n_base=parent.size
    if n_base < 512: parent = make_power(8192); n_base=8192
    candidates=[]
    add_candidate(candidates,'parent', parent)
    power1=make_power(n_base,1.0); add...
    power2=make_power(n_base,0.5)
    smooth1=make_smooth_random(rng,n_base,8,0.3)
    smooth2=make_smooth_random(rng,n_base,16,0.3)
    sin1=make_sin(n_base,1,0.25,0.0)
    sin3=make_sin(n_base,3,0.20,0.7)
    blend1=0.75*parent+0.25*power1; add
    blend2=0.80*parent+0.20*smooth1; add
    if n_base >=2048: add down2
    if n_base <=8192 and n_base*2<=MAX_N: add up2 (maybe interp)
    # If no parent, include exponentials
    if not parent_is_given:
       add exp fwd 1.5, exp bwd 1.5

```
       jump=a_best + smooth_noise(rng,n,scale)

    return best,bv
```

```
def do_jump(a_best, best_val, rng, n, scale, num=3):
    best_val = float(best_val)
    best_jump = None
    for _ in range(num):
        jump = a_best + smooth_noise(rng,n,scale)
        jump = project_simplex(jump,1.0)
        vj = float(conv_self(jump).max())
        if vj < best_val:
            best_val = vj
            best_jump = jump.copy()
    return best_jump, best_val
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The original problem is homogeneous, so all candidates are normalized to
sum 1 and the internal objective is 2*n*max(conv(a,a)).  The search is a
robust nonsmooth/local-global hybrid:

- multiple diverse candidates (parent, power laws, smooth Fourier randoms,
  blends, down/upsampled variants),
- projected subgradient steps from active convolution peaks,
- occasional global FFT-based log-sum-exp directions to handle plateaus,
- simulated-annealing-like smooth jumps when stuck,
- periodic smoothing steps,
- final verification with evaluate_sequence().
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from operator import itemgetter
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, fft, ifft, next_fast_len


def normalize_one(a):
    """Clip to non-negative finite values and normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def conv_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT, scaled to have sum exactly 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = next_fast_len(out_len)
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(A * A, fsize)[:out_len]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    total = float(np.sum(c))
    if total > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(total):
        c *= 1.0 / total
    return c


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    feasible = w > (cum - S) / idx
    if not np.any(feasible):
        theta = S / float(n)
    else:
        pos = int(np.where(feasible)[0].max())
        theta = (cum[pos] - S) / float(pos + 1)
    u = v - theta
    u = np.maximum(u, 0.0)
    su = float(np.sum(u))
    if not np.isfinite(su) or su <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(su - S) > 1e-12:
        u *= S / su
    return u


def top_indices(c, k):
    """Indices of the k largest entries of c, sorted descending."""
    m = int(c.size)
    k = int(min(max(1, k), m))
    if k >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    if k == 1:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    order = np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
    return idx[order].astype(np.int64)


def grad_from_peaks(a, idx, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k * c_k where c = a*a (linear convolution).
    For one lag k, d c_k / d a_i = 2 a_{k-i} on the valid range.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(a.size)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    maxk = 2 * n - 2
    for kk, ww in zip(idx, w):
        k = int(kk)
        w = float(ww)
        if w == 0.0 or k < 0 or k > maxk:
            continue
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        js = k - lo
        je = k - hi
        seg = a[je:js + 1][::-1]
        g[lo:hi + 1] += (2.0 * w) * seg
    return g


def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    """Softmax weights over convolution coefficients, with sum 1."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size == 0:
        return None
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return None
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    ew = np.exp(z)
    ew = np.nan_to_num(ew, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(ew))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return ew / s


def grad_weights_fft(a, w):
    """Gradient using an arbitrary non-negative weight array w over lags."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(a.size)
    if w.size != 2 * n - 1 or n == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    fsize = next_fast_len(w.size + n - 1)
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return g


def smooth_noise(rng, n, relative_scale):
    """Zero-mean low-frequency noise with per-element std ~ relative_scale/sqrt(n)."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    half = n // 2 + 1
    m = max(2, min(half, 16))
    f = np.zeros(half, dtype=np.float64)
    f[1:m] = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, max(0, m - 1))
    z = irfft(f, n)
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    sd = float(np.std(z))
    if sd <= 1e-300:
        z = rng.normal(0.0, relative_scale / np.sqrt(n), n)
    else:
        z *= (float(relative_scale) / np.sqrt(n)) / sd
    return z


def do_jump(a_best, best_val, rng, n, scale, num=3):
    """Try a few smooth random jumps from a_best and return the best one."""
    a_best = np.asarray(a_best, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(a_best.size)
    best_jump = None
    best_jump_val = np.inf
    for _ in range(max(1, int(num))):
        noise = smooth_noise(rng, n, float(scale))
        if noise is None:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(scale) / np.sqrt(n), n)
        jump = a_best + noise
        jump = project_simplex(jump, 1.0)
        v = float(np.max(conv_self(jump)))
        if np.isfinite(v) and v < best_jump_val:
            best_jump_val = v
            best_jump = jump.copy()
    return best_jump, float(best_jump_val)


def make_power(n, alpha=1.0, p=0.5):
    """Normalized negative-power-law start, good for continuous-style starts."""
    n = int(n)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = (t + max(1e-3, float(alpha))) ** (-float(p))
    return normalize_one(a)


def make_exponential(n, alpha=1.0, fwd=True):
    n = int(n)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    if fwd:
        a = np.exp(float(alpha) * t)
    else:
        a = np.exp(float(alpha) * (1.0 - t))
    return normalize_one(a)


def make_sin(n, m=1, amp=0.3, phase=0.0):
    n = int(n)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t + float(phase))
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return normalize_one(a)


def make_smooth_random(rng, n, nfreq=8, amp=0.35):
    n = int(n)
    half = n // 2 + 1
    m = max(2, min(half, int(nefreq) + 1))
    f = np.zeros(half, dtype=np.float64)
    f[1:m] = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, max(0, m - 1))
    z = irfft(f, n)
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    sd = float(np.std(z))
    if sd > 1e-300:
        z = float(amp) * z / sd
    else:
        z = 0.0
    a = 1.0 + z
    return normalize_one(a)


def downsample(a, factor):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if factor <= 1:
        return normalize_one(a)
    return normalize_one(a[0::factor])


def interp_seq(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    old_n = int(a.size)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 16:
        return normalize_one(np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return normalize_one(a)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    return normalize_one(np.interp(xn, xo, a))


def get_parent():
    """Access height_sequence_1 safely, if present."""
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if p.size >= 32 and np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-8:
        return normalize_one(p)
    return None


def add_candidate(cands, name, arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size >= 32:
        arr = normalize_one(arr)
        cands.append((name, arr.copy()))


def run_hill(a_init, rng, limit_time, Kmax=48, explore=0.1, tag='hill'):
    """
    Time-budgeted local search.

    It mixes:
    - active-peak subgradient descent,
    - smoothed log-sum-exp descending directions,
    - periodic smoothing,
    - annealed smooth restart jumps.

    Returns (best_array_normalized_to_sum_1, internal_objective).
    """
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(a_init.size)
    a = normalize_one(a_init.copy())

    c = conv_self(a)
    val = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_val = val

    base_alpha = 0.2
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0.0
    last_best_time = time.monotonic()

    print("[{}] n={} start obj={:.10f}".format(tag, n, 2.0 * n * val), flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < limit_time - 0.1:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= limit_time:
            break

        c = conv_self(a)
        val = float(np.max(c))
        it += 1

        if val < best_val - 1e-13:
            best_val = val
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            last_best_time = now
            base_alpha = min(1.5, base_alpha * 1.05 + 0.005)

        if it % 200 == 0 or (now - last_print) > 30.0:
            print("[{}] it={} obj={:.10f} best={:.10f} alpha={:.4g} stall={}".format(
                tag, it, 2.0 * n * val, 2.0 * n * best_val, base_alpha, stall
            ), flush=True)
            last_print = now

        # Annealed jump if the best has not moved for a while or local stall is large.
        time_since_best = now - last_best_time
        if time_since_best > 45.0 or stall > 1200:
            scale = min(0.08, 0.015 + 0.015 * min(3.0, float(stall) / 1500.0))
            if stall > 1200 or (time_since_best > 60.0 and rng.random() < 0.3):
                jarr, jval = do_jump(best_a, best_val, rng, n, scale, num=5)
                temp = 1e-3 + 5e-4 * min(2.0, float(stall) / 1500.0)
                if jarr is not None and np.isfinite(jval) and jval < best_val * (1.0 + temp):
                    a = jarr.copy()
                    val = jval
                    stall = 0
                    base_alpha = 0.1
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    val = best_val
                    stall = 0

        # Choose descent direction.
        lse_prob = 0.03 + min(0.45, 0.0005 * float(stall)) + 0.2 * float(explore)
        use_lse = False
        if rng.random() < min(0.8, lse_prob):
            beta_factor = 25.0 + 10.0 * min(20.0, float(stall) / 250.0)
            w = softmax_weights(c, beta_factor * float(n))
            if w is not None:
                use_lse = True

        if use_lse:
            g = grad_weights_fft(a, w)
        else:
            K = int(min(Kmax, max(2, 2 + int(stall) // 200)))
            if K < 16 and stall > 200:
                maxv = float(np.max(c))
                close_cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(c >= maxv - 1e-10))
                if close_cnt > K:
                    K = int(min(Kmax, close_cnt))
            idx = top_indices(c, K)
            sp = c[idx]
            r = rng.random()
            if r < 0.35:
                w = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
                w[0] = 1.0
            elif r < 0.65:
                s = float(np.sum(sp))
                if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                    w = sp / s
                else:
                    w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
            else:
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
                else:
                    w = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64))
            g = grad_from_peaks(a, idx, w)

        gt = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gt))
        if gnorm < 1e-13:
            v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
        else:
            v = -gt

        neg = v < 0.0
        if np.any(neg):
            amax = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-v[neg])))
        else:
            amax = 1.0
        use_projection = (amax < 1e-8)
        alpha = base_alpha
        improved = False
        attempt = 0

        for attempt in range(10):
            if not use_projection:
                if alpha > 0.95 * amax:
                    alpha = 0.95 * amax
                if alpha <= 0.0:
                    alpha = 1e-8
                cand = a + alpha * v
                mc = float(np.min(cand))
                if mc < -1e-12:
                    cand = project_simplex(cand, 1.0)
                    use_projection = True
                else:
                    ss = float(np.sum(cand))
                    if mc < 0.0:
                        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                        ss = float(np.sum(cand))
                    if ss > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ss) and abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-10:
                        cand = cand / ss
            else:
                cand = project_simplex(a + alpha * v, 1.0)

            cn = conv_self(cand)
            valn = float(np.max(cn))
            if valn < val - 1e-13:
                a = cand
                val = valn
                improved = True
                break
            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < 1e-10:
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if attempt == 0:
                base_alpha = min(2.0, base_alpha * 1.25 + 0.005)
            else:
                base_alpha = min(2.0, base_alpha * 1.05)
        else:
            stall += 1
            base_alpha *= 0.85
            if base_alpha < 1e-6:
                base_alpha = 1e-6

        # Periodic smoothing: removes high-frequency roughness if it helps.
        if it % 300 == 0 and n > 32:
            wsm = max(3, min(127, n // 16))
            if wsm % 2 == 0:
                wsm += 1
            kernel = np.ones(wsm, dtype=np.float64) / float(wsm)
            sm = np.convolve(a, kernel, mode='same')
            sm = normalize_one(sm)
            vsm = float(np.max(conv_self(sm)))
            tol = max(1e-13, 1e-11 * max(1.0, float(val)))
            if vsm < val - tol:
                a = sm
                val = vsm
                stall = 0
            elif vsm < best_val * (1.0 + 1e-4) and rng.random() < 0.2:
                a = sm
                val = vsm
                stall = 0

        # If we drifted too far below the record, reset to the best record.
        if val > best_val * 1.02 and stall > 400:
            a = best_a.copy()
            val = best_val
            stall = 0
            base_alpha = 0.2

    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * best_val
    print("[{}] finish obj={:.10f}".format(tag, obj), flush=True)
    return best_a, float(obj)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search function invoked by the evaluator.
    Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(max(0.0, budget_s))

    if budget_s < 90.0:
        parent = get_parent()
        if parent is not None:
            if parent.size > 8192:
                parent = interp_seq(parent, 8192)
            return [float(x) for x in parent]
        arr = normalize_one(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        return [float(x) for x in arr]

    deadline = start + budget_s - 30.0
    MAX_N = 16384

    parent = get_parent()
    parent_given = parent is not None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > MAX_N:
        parent = interp_seq(parent, MAX_N)

    if parent is None:
        parent = make_power(8192, alpha=1.0, p=0.5)
        parent_given = False

    if parent.size < 512:
        parent = make_power(8192, alpha=1.0, p=0.5)
        parent_given = False

    n_base = int(parent.size)
    if n_base > 16384:
        parent = interp_seq(parent, 16384)
        n_base = 16384

    cands = []
    add_candidate(cands, 'parent', parent)

    power1 = make_power(n_base, alpha=1.0, p=0.5)
    power2 = make_power(n_base, alpha=0.5, p=0.5)
    add_candidate(cands, 'power1', power1)
    add_candidate(cands, 'power05', power2)

    smooth1 = make_smooth_random(rng, n_base, nfreq=8, amp=0.30)
    smooth2 = make_smooth_random(rng, n_base, nfreq=16, amp=0.30)
    add_candidate(cands, 'smooth1', smooth1)
    add_candidate(cands, 'smooth2', smooth2)

    add_candidate(cands, 'sin1', make_sin(n_base, 1, 0.25, 0.0))
    add_candidate(cands, 'sin3', make_sin(n_base, 3, 0.20, 0.7))

    if parent_given:
        add_candidate(cands, 'blend_power', 0.75 * parent + 0.25 * power1)
        add_candidate(cands, 'blend_smooth', 0.80 * parent + 0.20 * smooth1)
        if n_base >= 2048:
            add_candidate(cands, 'down2', downsample(parent, 2))
        if n_base <= 8192 and n_base * 2 <= MAX_N:
            add_candidate(cands, 'up2', interp_seq(parent, n_base * 2))
    else:
        add_candidate(cands, 'exp_f', make_exponential(n_base, 1.5, fwd=True))
        add_candidate(cands, 'exp_b', make_exponential(n_base, 1.5, fwd=False))

    # Score initial candidates with fast FFT convolution.
    pooled = []
    for name, arr in cands:
        arr = normalize_one(arr)
        c = conv_self(arr)
        score = 2.0 * float(arr.size) * float(np.max(c))
        if np.isfinite(score):
            pooled.append((score, name, arr.copy()))

    if len(pooled) == 0:
        fallback = normalize_one(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    pooled.sort(key=itemgetter(0))

    # Exploration phase over diverse starts.
    if parent_given:
        explore_fraction = min(0.12, max(0.05, 120.0 / max(1.0, budget_s)))
        explore_limit = start + min(300.0, max(60.0, budget_s * explore_fraction))
    else:
        explore_fraction = min(0.45, max(0.25, 600.0 / max(1.0, budget_s)))
        explore_limit = start + min(1200.0, max(120.0, budget_s * explore_fraction))

    explore_limit = min(explore_limit, deadline - 60.0)

    starts = []
    for i in range(min(6, len(pooled))):
        starts.append((pooled[i][1], pooled[i][2]))

    exp_results = []
    for i in range(len(starts)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= explore_limit:
            break
        rem_explore = explore_limit - now
        if rem_explore < 30.0:
            break
        name, arr = starts[i]
        alloc = min(rem_explore, 120.0)
        if i == 0:
            alloc = min(rem_explore, 200.0)
        limit = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        a_opt, score_opt = run_hill(
            arr, rng, limit,
            Kmax=48, explore=0.25,
            tag='exp_{}_{}'.format(name, i)
        )
        if np.isfinite(score_opt):
            exp_results.append((score_opt, name + '_e{}'.format(i), a_opt))

    for item in exp_results:
        pooled.append(item)
    pooled.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    if len(pooled) > 10:
        pooled = pooled[:10]

    # Deep polishing, with the current best receiving the majority of time.
    if len(pooled) == 0:
        fallback = normalize_one(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    best_score, best_name, best_a = pooled[0]

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = deadline - now
    if remaining > 60.0:
        t1 = 0.45 * remaining
        limit1 = min(deadline, now + t1)
        a1, s1 = run_hill(
            best_a, rng, limit1,
            Kmax=48, explore=0.05,
            tag='deepA'
        )
        best_a = a1
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = s1
            best_name = 'deepA'
        now = time.monotonic()

    remaining = deadline - now
    if remaining > 30.0:
        alt_score = None
        alt_a = None
        for i in range(1, len(pooled)):
            cand_score = pooled[i][0]
            if np.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score < best_score + 0.02:
                alt_score = cand_score
                alt_a = pooled[i][2]
                break

        if alt_a is not None:
            t2 = min(0.20 * remaining, 300.0)
            limit2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + t2)
            a2, s2 = run_hill(
                alt_a, rng, limit2,
                Kmax=64, explore=0.30,
                tag='altA'
            )
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_a = a2
                best_name = 'altA'
        else:
            t2 = min(0.20 * remaining, 300.0)
            limit2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + t2)
            a2, s2 = run_hill(
                best_a, rng, limit2,
                Kmax=64, explore=0.15,
                tag='deepB'
            )
            best_a = a2
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_name = 'deepB'

    remaining = deadline - now
    if remaining > 20.0:
        t3 = min(0.95 * remaining, deadline - time.monotonic())
        limit3 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + t3)
        a3, s3 = run_hill(
            best_a, rng, limit3,
            Kmax=64, explore=0.08,
            tag='final'
        )
        best_a = a3
        if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score:
            best_score = s3
            best_name = 'final'

    # Final verification with evaluate_sequence on the best few internal candidates.
    final_candidates = [best_a]
    for i in range(min(4, len(pooled))):
        final_candidates.append(pooled[i][2])

    best_list = None
    best_eval = np.inf

    for arr in final_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if arr.size < 2:
            continue
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
            arr = normalize_one(np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64))
        ll = [float(x) for x in arr]
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence(ll)
        except Exception:
            val = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_eval:
            best_eval = val
            best_list = ll

    if best_list is None:
        # Safe fallback; should rarely be used if a candidate was finite internally.
        if best_a is not None and best_a.size >= 2:
            arr = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64), nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
                arr = normalize_one(arr)
            best_list = [float(x) for x in arr]
        else:
            best_list = [float(x) for x in normalize_one(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))]

    print("propose_candidate returning length={} verified_score={}".format(
        len(best_list),
        best_eval if np.isfinite(best_eval) else float('inf')
    ), flush=True)
    return best_list
```
